def optimize_logit_adam(p0, budget_s, rng, tau_ratio0=0.10, tau_ratio1=0.002, lr0=0.05, max_steps_per_phase=??):

    z=...; m/v; best_p; best_score=fast_score(p); cur_score...
    step=0; last_restart=0; no_improve=0
    while time<deadline:
       # dynamically set tau and lr maybe based on progress/time
       # maybe use a plan: tau = tau0*(tau1/tau0)^{fraction_time}; lr similar
       # but if stuck, perturb.
       tau = current*?; 
       score, grad = obj_grad(p,tau,L)
       update...
       p=softmax(z); step++; no_improve...
       if no_improve > threshold:
           if step-last_restart > min: add noise to best_p then set p,z, reset m/v, last_restart=step, no_improve=0, maybe increase tau, decrease lr.
    return best
```

High-dim objective_and_grad implementation detail:
```
def conv_core(p, L):
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P*P, L)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return P, c (and P maybe for gradient)
```
For gradient FFT W, recompute P? We can return P and use. In `score_grad(p, tau, L, K_direct=64)`:
- c, P.
- cmax. score_true=2n cmax.
- if tau <=0: weights exact top? else z.
- Determine mask. For memory, `z = (c - cmax) / tau; mask = z > -30` maybe. expz = np.exp(z[mask]) with zerod? If mask all false? no.
- K=mask.sum()
- if K <= K_direct: direct.
   wsexp = expz / expsum. indices = np.flatnonzero(mask)
   gp = np.zeros(n)
   for idx,w in zip(indices,wsw): if w<1e-12 continue; call add_row(gp, idx, w, p)
- else:
   W = np.zeros(2n-1); W[mask]=ewsw; Wref=W[::-1]
   H = irfft(P * rfft(Wref,L), L)[:2n-1]
   h = H[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
   gp = 2*h
- gp = finite, maybe clip large.
- mean = dot(p,gp)
- grad_z = p * (gp - mean)
- return score_true, grad_z
```

def fast_score(a):
  p=safe_normalize(a); if None return inf; n=p.size; L=next_pow2(2*n-1); P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0); cm=c.max(); if cm<=0 return inf? Actually if all zero returns inf. return 2*n*cm
```
Since p sum=1, cm positive. For binary safe.

`convolution_coeffs(p,L)`:
```
P = np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0); return P,c
```

`score_grad(p, tau, L, k_direct=32)`:
- P,c = conv.
- cmax = float(c.max())
- true_score = 2*n*cmax
- if not finite or cmax<=0 return inf, zeros
- if tau >0:
  z=(c-cmax)/tau
  # To avoid underflow, mask z > -40 maybe
  mask = z > -40.0
  if not np.any(mask): mask[argmax]=True
  expz = np.exp(z[mask])
  s=expz.sum()
  if not finite/s<=0: weights one-hot argmax
  weights_local=expz/s
  K=len(weights_local)
- else:
  # top 1? maybe K=16 within 1e-12? Simpler one-hot argmax.
  idx = int(np.argmax(c)); mask zeros; mask[idx]=1; weights_local=1.
- if K <= k_direct:
   gp=np.zeros(n)
   idxs=np.flatnonzero(mask)
   for ii in range(K):
      w=weights_local[ii]
      if w < 1e-15: continue
      k=int(idxs[ii])
      # add row
      i0 = k if k < n else n - 1? formula max(0,k-n+1)
      i0 = max(0, k - n + 1); i1 = min(n-1,k)
      if i0 <= i1:
         j0 = k - i1; j1 = k - i0
         # j0..j1 length i1-i0+1
         seg = p[j0:j1+1][::-1]
         gp[i0:i1+1] += (2.0*w)*seg
   # else FFT
   W=np.zeros(2*n-1)
   W[mask]=weights_local
   Wref=W[::-1]
   HW = np.fft.irfft(P * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)[:2*n-1]
   gp = 2.0 * HW[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
- gp=np.where(finite,gp,0)
- mean=float(np.dot(p,gp))
- grad = p * (gp - mean)
- # Clip grad for stability
- maxg=np.max(np.abs(grad)); if maxg>1e1: grad*=1e1/maxg; elif not finite: zeros
- return true_score, grad

Check direct gradient dimension: `seg = p[j0:j1+1][::-1]`: If j0=2,j1=4, seg length3. `gp[i0:i1+1] += seg * 2w`. Good. Note derivative factor includes 2. For weighted sum of c (weights sum1), grad wrt p = ∑w ∂c_k = ∑w 2 row. Good.
Mean subtraction: For objective on p sum1, gradient wrt z = p*(gp - mean). But if we include factor 2n constant? Omitted. Good.

`adam_phase` in detail:
```
def adam_phase(p0, deadline, rng, tau_ratio0, tau_ratio1, lr0, lr1, k_direct=32, start_phase_duration=None, label=""):
    p = safe_normalize(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    n=p.size; L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    phase_start=time.monotonic()
    phase_len=max(1.0, deadline-phase_start)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
    m=zeros; v=zeros; beta...; t=0
    best=p.copy(); best_s=fast_score(p); cur=float(best_s)
    no_improve=0; step=0; last_noise=0; current_tau_override=None
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       now=time.monotonic(); frac=(now-phase_start)/phase_len; frac=min(1,max(0,frac))
       tau_arr = (tau_ratio0 + (tau_ratio1-tau_ratio0)*frac) / n
       if tau_arr > 0 and not finite: tau_arr=1.0/n
       lr = lr0 + (lr1-lr0)*frac
       score, grad = score_grad(p, tau_arr, L, k_direct)
       if not finite(score): break
       cur=score
       if score < best_s - 1e-12:
          best_s=score; best=p.copy(); no_improve=0
       else: no_improve+=1
       # maybe if current much worse than best, pull back
       if no_improve > 4000 and score > best_s * (1.02) and step > 1000:
          p = best.copy(); z=log...; m*=0.1; v*=0.1; no_improve=0
       # Add periodic noise to escape: if no_improve>10000, add noise to best and reset
       if no_improve > 15000 and (step-last_noise)>20000:
          noise_std = 0.2 if frac<0.5 else 0.1
          pp = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise_std, n))
          pp = safe_normalize(pp)
          p=pp; z=...; m*=0.3; v*=0.3; no_improve=0; last_noise=step
       # grad update
       maxg=np.max(np.abs(grad))
       if maxg>0 and finite:
          if maxg>1e-2: grad *= 1e-2/maxg? # This scaling via Adam cancels? But if maxg huge, clip.
          t +=1; m=...; v=...; b1/b2; z = z - lr*(m/b1)/(sqrt(v/b2)+eps)
          z = np.where(finite,z,0); z -= z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
          p = stable_softmax(z)
       step += 1
       if step % 20000 ==0: print(label, step, cur, best_s)
    return best,best_s
```

```
def theta_adam_phase(theta0, B, deadline, rng, tau_ratio0, tau_ratio1, lr0, lr1, k_direct=32, label="theta"):
    theta=np.asarray(theta0).copy()
    n,Bdim=B.shape
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    # init p & score
    p=stable_softmax(B@theta); best_p=p; best_s=fast_score(p); best_theta=theta.copy()
    m,v zeros(Bdim); step=0
    phase_start = time.monotonic(); phase_len=deadline-phase_start
    while time<deadline:
       frac=...
       tau=.../n; lr=...
       eta=B@theta
       p=stable_softmax(eta)
       score, grad_eta = score_grad_eta(theta,B,tau,L,k_direct) # or compute score_grad(p) then B.T
       if score<best_s: update best_theta
       no_improve...
       update m/v/theta
       p=stable_softmax(B@theta)
       step...
    return best_p,best_s,best_theta
```
To avoid computing p twice, `theta_score_grad` computes p and grad. We'll implement:
```
def theta_score_grad(theta,B,tau,L,k_direct):
   eta=B@theta; p=stable_softmax(eta); score,grad_eta_p = score_grad(p,tau,L,k_direct); grad_theta=B.T @ grad_eta_p; return p,score,grad_theta
```
`score_grad` returns grad_z (eta-space). Good.
When updating theta, no need p. For best_p use p if improved.

```
def resize_sequence(seq,m):
 p=safe_normalize(seq)
 if p is None: return ones/m
 n=len(p)
 if n==m return p
 if n==1 return full
 x_old=np.arange(n)/(n-1) if n>1 else [0]
 x_new=np.arange(m)/(m-1) if m>1 else [0]
 out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
 out=np.maximum(out,0); s=sum; return out/s
```

Helper `next_pow2`.

`safe_normalize(a)`:
```
def safe_normalize(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300: return None
    return arr/s
```

`stable_softmax(z)`:
```
def stable_softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size==0: return np.array([],dtype=np.float64)
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -50.0)
    z=z - z.max()
    z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0.0:
       return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
    return e/s
```

`score_grad`:
```
def score_grad(p, tau, L, k_direct=32):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.inf, np.zeros(0)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax<=0.0:
       return np.inf, np.zeros(n)
    true_score=2.0*float(n)*cmax
    if tau <= 0.0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(c))
       gp=np.zeros(n)
       # add row for idx weight 1
       gp = row...
    else:
       tol=40.0*tau
       mask = c > (cmax - tol)
       if not np.any(mask):
           mask=np.zeros(c.shape,bool); mask[int(np.argmax(c))]=True
       zsel=(c[mask]-cmax)/tau
       expz=np.exp(zsel)
       s=float(expz.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
           idxs=np.flatnonzero(mask); ... reset
       else: weights=expz/s
       K=len(weights)
       if K <= k_direct:
          gp=np.zeros(n)
          idxs=np.flatnonzero(mask)
          for ii in range(K):
             w=float(weights[ii])
             if w < 1e-15: continue
             k=int(idxs[ii])
             i0 = 0 if k < n-1 else k-n+1? Let's use max.
             i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
             i1 = min(n-1, k)
             if i0 <= i1:
                j0 = k - i1
                j1 = k - i0
                gp[i0:i1+1] += (2.0*w) * (p[j0:j1+1][::-1])
       else:
          W=np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.float64)
          W[mask]=weights
          Wref=W[::-1]
          HW=np.fft.irfft(P*np.fft.rfft(Wref,L), L)[:2*n-1]
          gp=2.0*HW[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    gp=np.where(np.isfinite(gp),gp,0.0)
    mean=float(np.dot(p,gp))
    grad=p*(gp-mean)
    grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad),grad,0.0)
    # If grad all zero, no update
    return true_score, grad
```
Need factor for tau? derivative of logsumexp uses weights, no tau factor (because logsumexp derivative weights times derivative c). Correct. We omitted score's 2n. Fine.

Should weights be normalized over mask truncated? If mask excludes small coefficients, the smooth approximation is truncated; gradient may be slightly off but fine. Exponent sum over mask; ignored tail negligible (z<-40). Good.

For tau small, weights sum1. Good.

Maybe if cmax very small, tol=40tau could exceed cmax and include all, but tau small; okay. If tau larger than cmax, tol >cmax, mask all; weights positive for all. Fine.

   if i0<=i1:
       j0=k-i1; j1=k-i0

def random_theta(D, rng, scale=1.0):
   if D==0: return np.zeros(0)
   base = np.sqrt(np.arange(1,D+1,dtype=np.float64))
   th = rng.normal(0.0, scale/base, D)
   # emphasize low modes
   if D>0:
       th[0] *= rng.choice([1,-1])? np.random Generator has `rng.integers`? Use if rng.random()<0.5 else -1. And uniform.
   # Maybe clip
   return np.clip(th, -15,15)

def theta_score_grad(theta, B, tau, L, k_direct):

def theta_adam_phase(theta0, B, deadline, rng, tau_ratio0=0.12, tau_ratio1=0.02, lr0=0.08, lr1=0.03, k_direct=32, label="theta"):
    theta=np.asarray(theta0,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n=B.shape[0]; D=B.shape[1]
    if n==0: return None,inf,theta
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    p=stable_softmax(B.dot(theta))
    best_p=p.copy(); best_s=fast_score(p); best_theta=theta.copy()
    m=np.zeros(D); v=np.zeros(D); bc1=bc2=0? step counter for bias.
    step=0; no_improve=0; phase_start=time.monotonic(); phase_len=max(1e-6, deadline-phase_start)
    beta1=.9; beta2=.999; eps=1e-8
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       now=time.monotonic(); frac=min(1,max(0,(now-phase_start)/phase_len))
       tau=(tau_ratio0+(tau_ratio1-tau_ratio0)*frac)/n
       lr=lr0+(lr1-lr0)*frac
       p,score,gt=theta_score_grad(theta,B,tau,L,k_direct)
       if not np.isfinite(score): break
       if score < best_s - 1e-12:
           best_s=score; best_p=p.copy(); best_theta=theta.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 8000 and score > best_s*1.02:
           theta = best_theta + rng.normal(0,0.05,np.sqrt(np.arange(1,D+1)))? Could simple.
           m*=0.2; v*=0.2; no_improve=0
       maxg=np.max(np.abs(gt)) if D else 0
       if maxg>1e-300 and np.isfinite(maxg):
          if maxg>1e3: gt*=1e3/maxg
          step+=1
          m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*gt
          v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*(gt*gt)
          b1=1-beta1**step; b2=1-beta2**step
          theta=theta - lr*(m/b1)/(np.sqrt(v/b2)+eps)
          theta=np.where(np.isfinite(theta),theta,0.0)
          # keep theta bounded to avoid eta overflow; softmax clips anyway but B dot maybe huge.
          maxt=np.max(np.abs(theta)); if maxt>50: theta*=50/maxt
       step_count? Use step for both.
       if step % 2000 ==0: print(label, step, score, best_s)
    return best_p,best_s,best_theta

def adam_phase(p0, deadline, rng, tau_ratio0=0.1, tau_ratio1=0.03, lr0=0.04, lr1=0.02, k_direct=32, label="adam"):

This is nonnegative, max 1.5 avg, min0.5. It is symmetric around center? cos(π t') with t' 0..1: min at endpoints, max center. Good. It is smooth. But `np.sqrt` on array length L/2+1; need enforce Hermitian for full? irfft requires Hermitian conjugates for negative half. Since C_r[k] and C_r[L-k]=conj; principal sqrt: sqrt(conj(z)) not always conj(sqrt(z)) if branch cut. We can symmetrize by constructing full complex array maybe. Simpler: Use `np.fft.irfft` with rfft of c_t and then set `R = np.exp(0.5 * np.log(C_r))`? Still branch. We can define magnitude sqrt and phase half with an angular unwrapping to ensure A real. The resulting A may not be real? Inverse of e^{-iπk(n-1)/L} times real amplitude gives a shifted sequence; may be real if amplitude even. ```
def make_initial_seeds(best_theta, D, rng):
    seeds=[]
    if D==0: return [np.zeros(0)]*4? But D likely >0.
    seeds.append(best_theta.copy())
    seeds.append(np.zeros(D))
    for i in range(6): seeds.append(random_theta(D,rng,1.0))
    # hand
    t=np.zeros(D)
    if D>0: t[0]=1.2
    seeds.append(t); seeds.append(-t)
    if D>1: t2=copy; t2[1]=-0.8; seeds...
    etc.
    # jitter best
    for i in range(6): seeds.append(jittered_theta(best_theta,D,rng,0.4))
    return seeds
```

`jittered_theta`:
```
def jittered_theta(theta,D,rng,std=0.5):
   if D==0: return theta
   base=np.sqrt(np.arange(1,D+1))
   noise=std*rng.normal(size=D)/base
   return theta+noise
```

In `low_dim_search`, after each seed, if improved, update `base_theta` for generating new. To avoid duplicates, okay.

```
def low_dim_search(parent_p, deadline, rng, n, B, D):
    if D==0: return None
    p0=resize_sequence(parent_p,n) if parent_p is not None else None
    if p0 is None: p0=np.ones(n)/n
    best_p=p0; best_s=fast_score(p0)
    best_theta=project_log_to_basis(p0,B)
    seeds=make_initial_seeds(best_theta,D,rng)
    seed_idx=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    total=max(1.0,deadline-start)
    # individual length max 8, min 2
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if seed_idx >= len(seeds):
            # generate from current best
            seeds.append(jittered_theta(best_theta,D,rng,0.4))
            seeds.append(random_theta(D,rng,1.2))
        theta=seeds[seed_idx]; seed_idx+=1
        if theta is None: continue
        # allocate dur: if many seeds left, smaller; else up to 8
        remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if remaining <=0: break
        dur=min(8.0, max(2.0, remaining/ max(1, min(len(seeds)-seed_idx+1, 8))))? Vehicle
        # Simpler: dur = 4.0 if len(seeds)-seed_idx > 10 else 6.0
        if len(seeds) - seed_idx > 12: dur=3.0
        elif len(seeds)-seed_idx > 4: dur=5.0
        else: dur=8.0
        end=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+dur)
        p,s,th=theta_adam_phase(theta,B,end,rng,0.15,0.03,0.08,0.03,32,"theta")
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best_p=p.copy(); best_s=s; best_theta=th.copy()
            print("low improve", best_s)
    return best_p,best_s,best_theta
```

Let's implement `lbfz_attempt` more controlled:
```
def lbfz_attempt(p0, deadline, rng, tau_ratio, maxiter=80, maxfun=1200, k_direct=32):
    p=safe_normalize(p0)
    if p is None: return None,inf
    n=p.size; L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
    tau=tau_ratio/n
    start=time.monotonic()
    option={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'disp':False}
    try:
       res=scipy_minimize(fixed_scipy_objective, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(tau,n,L,k_direct), options=option)
    except Exception:
       res=None
    if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
       p2=stable_softmax(np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64))
       s2=fast_score(p2)
       if np.isfinite(s2): return p2,s2
    return p, fast_score(p)
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except: budget=2400.0
    budget=max(60.0,min(budget, 2400.0? no, if larger?))
    deadline=start + max(30.0, budget - 60.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    N_HIGH = 8192
    best_p=None; best_s=np.inf
    # load
    try:
       hs=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       hs=...; if size>0: 
           p=resize_sequence(hs,N_HIGH); s=fast_score(p); if finite best
    except Exception: pass
    if best_p is None: best_p=np.ones(N_HIGH)/N_HIGH; best_s=2.0
    print("initial", best_s)
    # quick variants of previous? maybe later.
    now=time.monotonic(); remaining=deadline-now
    # low dim
    if remaining > 240:
        low_n = 4096 if budget>1800 else 2048
        D=40 if low_n>=4096 else 32
        B_low=cheb_basis(low_n,D)
        low_deadline=start + min(180.0, max(80.0, remaining*0.12))
        # But low_deadline may exceed deadline; use min(deadline, time.monotonic()+low_budget)
        low_budget = min(180.0, max(60.0, remaining*0.12))
        if low_budget > 30:
            low_p, low_s, low_th = low_dim_search(best_p, time.monotonic()+low_budget, rng, low_n, B_low, D)
            if low_p is not None:
                p=resize_sequence(low_p,N_HIGH); s=fast_score(p); update
        print("after low", best_s)
    # L-BFGS early
    if HAVE_MINIMIZE and time.monotonic() + 60 < deadline - 200:
       p,s=lbfz_attempt(best_p, deadline, rng, 0.12, maxiter=80, maxfun=1200)
       if p is not None: update resize
       print("after lbfz early", best_s)
    # high phases
    high_end=deadline-75
    if high_end > time.monotonic() + 120:
       h_start=time.monotonic(); h_len=high_end-h_start
       e1=h_start+0.38*h_len
       p1,s1=adam_phase(best_p,e1,rng,0.15,0.05,0.045,0.025,32,"h1")
       update
       # Phase2 from noise
       noise_parent = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.15,N_HIGH)); noise_parent=safe_normalize(noise_parent)
       if noise_parent is None: noise_parent=best_p.copy()
       e2=h_start+0.70*h_len
       p2,s2=adam_phase(noise_parent,e2,rng,0.08,0.02,0.035,0.018,32,"h2")
       update
       # Phase3 from best with small noise?
       if time.monotonic() < high_end - 30:
          p3parent = best_p.copy()
          # maybe if best_s>1.55, add noise 0.1 else 0.05
          noise_std = 0.10 if best_s > 1.55 else 0.04
          p3parent = safe_normalize(best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,noise_std,N_HIGH)))
          p3,s3=adam_phase(p3parent,high_end,rng,0.03,0.006,0.022,0.008,24,"h3")
          update
       print("after high", best_s)
    # late L-BFGS
    if HAVE_MINIMIZE and time.monotonic() + 30 < deadline - 70:
       p,s=lbfz_attempt(best_p, deadline, rng, 0.03, maxiter=80, maxfun=1200, k_direct=24)
       update
    # final
    final_seq = finish_sequence(best_p, rng, deadline)
    return final_seq
```

Define `finish_sequence(best_p, rng, deadline)`:
```
def finish_sequence(best_p, rng, deadline):
   p=safe_normalize(best_p)
   if p is None: p=np.ones(1024)/1024
   N=p.size
   cands=[]; scores=[]
   consider_candidate(cands,scores,p)
   # scales
   scales=(1.0, 5.0, 20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0, 1000000.0)
   for S in scales:
       arr=np.minimum(p*S,1000.0)
       consider_candidate(...)
   # trunc/binary
   fracs=(0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8,0.9)
   for f in fracs:
      m=int(N*f)
      if 0<m<N:
         thr=np.partition(p, N-m)[N-m]
         arr=np.where(p>=thr,p,0.0)
         consider_candidate(arr)
      m=int(N*f)
      if 0<m<N:
         b=np.where(p>=thr,1.0,0.0)
         consider_candidate(b)
   # if N>=8191 and time? add legendre maybe
   if N >= 8100 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
      arr=legendre_candidate(8191)
      if arr is not None: consider_candidate(arr)
   # Fallback uniform 1024
   consider_candidate(np.ones(1024)/1024)
   if not cands: return [1.0/1024]*1024
   # sort
   arr_scores = np.array(scores, dtype=np.float64)
   order = np.argsort(arr_scores)
   top=[]
   for idx in order:
       if not np.isfinite(arr_scores[idx]): continue
       top.append(cands[int(idx)])
       if len(top)>=4: break
   # actual validation
   final_seq=None; final_true=np.inf
   eval_limit = 2
   for arr in top:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       if arr.size > 16000 and len(final_seq is not None)? We can do first even if >16000 maybe. Let's just top 2.
       lst=[float(x) for x in np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)]
       try:
          sc=evaluate_sequence(lst)
       except Exception:
          sc=np.inf
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_true:
          final_true=float(sc); final_seq=lst
       eval_limit-=1
   if final_seq is None:
       final_seq=[float(x) for x in np.clip(top[0],0.0,1000.0)]
   return final_seq
```

```
def legendre_candidate(n_target=8191):
   # find prime? Fixed 8191.
   p=8191
   a=np.zeros(p)
   a[0]=1.0
   half=(p-1)//2
   for i in range(1,p):
       if pow(i, half, p) == 1:
           a[i]=1.0
   return a
```

`low_dim_search`: If parent_p is None -> uniform. Build B. If D==0 skip. It may run many theta. Need avoid if deadline already passed. Use `budget = max(0,deadline - now)`. If <30 return parent resized.

```
def low_dim_search(parent_p, deadline, rng, n, B, D):
   p0=resize_sequence(parent_p,n) if parent_p is not None else None
   if p0 is None: p0=np.ones(n)/n
   best_p=p0; best_s=fast_score(p0); best_theta=project_log_to_basis(p0,B) if D else zeros
   if time.monotonic() >= deadline or D==0: return best_p,best_s,best_theta
   seeds=make_initial_seeds(best_theta,D,rng)
   idx=0; start=time.monotonic()
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if idx >= len(seeds):
          seeds.append(jittered_theta(best_theta,D,rng,0.5))
          seeds.append(random_theta(D,rng,1.3))
       theta=seeds[idx]; idx+=1
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if remaining <=0: break
       if idx % 10 ==0: print("low seed", idx, "best", best_s)
       # dur: leave at least 5s for at least one
       if remaining > 120:
          dur = 4.0 if idx < 10 and len(seeds)-idx > 10 else min(8.0, remaining*0.15)
       else:
          dur = remaining*0.4
       dur = max(2.0, min(dur, remaining-1.0)) if remaining>3 else remaining
       end = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+dur)
       p,s,th = theta_adam_phase(theta,B,end,rng,0.18,0.04,0.10,0.04,32,"theta")
       if p is not None and np.isfinite(s) and s<best_s:
          best=p; best_s=s; best_theta=th
          print("low improve", best_s)
   return best_p,best_s,best_theta
```
This might run many 4s seeds in 120s (30). Good.

def legendre_candidate(p=8191):
    a=np.zeros(p)
    a[0]=1.0
    half=(p-1)//2

def consider_candidate(cands, scores, arr):

```python
"""
Hybrid multimethod search for low-peak non-negative convolution sequences.

Components:
- Fast FFT surrogate for evaluate_sequence (scale invariant unless clipping).
- Chebyshev/log-density low-dimensional global search.
- High-dimensional log-domain Adam optimizer with adaptive smoothed max.
- Optional L-BFGS refinement with a smooth maximum objective.
- Final diversification: clipping, power/mixing transforms, length upsampling,
  and a sparse Legendre-residue candidate.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
    HAVE_MINIMIZE = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_MINIMIZE = False


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def safe_normalize(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return arr / s


def stable_softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -50.0)
    z = z - z.max()
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def fast_score(a):
    p = safe_normalize(a)
    if p is None:
        return np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cm)


def add_transition_row(target, a, k, weight, n):
    i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
    i1 = min(n - 1, k)
    if i0 <= i1:
        j0 = k - i1
        j1 = k - i0
        target[i0:i1 + 1] += (2.0 * weight) * (a[j0:j1 + 1][::-1])


def score_grad(p, tau, L, k_direct=32):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    true_score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax

    if tau <= 0.0:
        gp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        add_transition_row(gp, p, idx, 1.0, n)
    else:
        tol = 40.0 * float(tau)
        mask = c > (cmax - tol)
        if not np.any(mask):
            mask = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=bool)
            mask[int(np.argmax(c))] = True

        zsel = (c[mask] - cmax) / float(tau)
        expz = np.exp(zsel)
        s = float(expz.sum())

        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            gp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            add_transition_row(gp, p, idx, 1.0, n)
        else:
            weights = expz / s
            K = int(weights.size)
            if K <= int(k_direct):
                gp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                idxs = np.flatnonzero(mask)
                for ii in range(K):
                    w = float(weights[ii])
                    if w <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    k = int(idxs[ii])
                    add_transition_row(gp, p, k, w, n)
            else:
                W = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
                W[mask] = weights
                Wref = W[::-1]
                HW = np.fft.irfft(P * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)[:2 * n - 1]
                gp = 2.0 * HW[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

    gp = np.where(np.isfinite(gp), gp, 0.0)
    mean_g = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    grad = p * (gp - mean_g)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

    maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(maxg):
        grad = np.zeros_like(grad)
    elif maxg > 1e6:
        grad *= (1e6 / maxg)

    return true_score, grad


def try_update(best_p, best_s, cand, target_n):
    if cand is None:
        return best_p, best_s
    arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return best_p, best_s
    if arr.size != target_n:
        arr = resize_sequence(arr, int(target_n))
    else:
        arr = safe_normalize(arr)
    if arr is None:
        return best_p, best_s
    s = fast_score(arr)
    if np.isfinite(s) and (best_p is None or s < best_s):
        return arr, float(s)
    return best_p, best_s


def resize_sequence(seq, m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    p = safe_normalize(seq)
    if p is None:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    n = p.size
    if n == m:
        return p.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return out / s


def cheb_basis(n, D):
    n = int(n)
    D = int(D)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty((0, max(0, D)), dtype=np.float64)
    if D <= 0:
        return np.empty((n, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    out = np.empty((n, D), dtype=np.float64)
    out[:, 0] = t
    if D >= 2:
        Tm0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        Tm1 = t.copy()
        for m in range(1, D):
            Tm2 = 2.0 * t * Tm1 - Tm0
            out[:, m] = Tm2
            Tm0, Tm1 = Tm1, Tm2
    return out


def project_log_to_basis(p, B):
    D = B.shape[1]
    if D == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y -= y.mean()
    y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)
    G = B.T.dot(B) + 1e-8 * np.eye(D, dtype=np.float64)
    rhs = B.T.dot(y)
    try:
        theta = np.linalg.solve(G, rhs)
    except Exception:
        theta = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    return np.clip(theta, -20.0, 20.0)


def random_theta(D, rng, amp=1.0):
    D = int(D)
    if D <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    scale = amp / (np.arange(1, D + 1, dtype=np.float64) ** 0.7)
    th = rng.normal(0.0, 0.8 * scale, D)
    sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
    th[0] *= sign * rng.uniform(0.5, 1.6)
    return np.clip(th, -8.0, 8.0)


def jittered_theta(theta, D, rng, std=0.5):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    D = int(D)
    if D <= 0 or theta.size != D:
        return theta.copy()
    scale = std / (np.arange(1, D + 1, dtype=np.float64) ** 0.7)
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, D) * scale
    return np.clip(theta + noise, -20.0, 20.0)


def make_initial_seeds(best_theta, D, rng):
    D = int(D)
    if D <= 0:
        return [np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)]

    seeds = []
    seeds.append(np.asarray(best_theta, dtype=np.float64).copy())
    seeds.append(np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64))

    for _ in range(6):
        seeds.append(random_theta(D, rng, 1.0))
    for _ in range(6):
        seeds.append(jittered_theta(best_theta, D, rng, 0.55))

    if D > 0:
        t = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        t[0] = 1.4
        seeds.append(t)
        seeds.append(-t)

    if D > 1:
        t = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        t[0] = -0.8
        t[1] = 1.1
        seeds.append(t)
        seeds.append(-t)

    if D > 2:
        t = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        t[0] = 1.2
        t[2] = -0.9
        seeds.append(t)
        seeds.append(-t)

    if D > 4:
        t = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        t[0] = -1.0
        t[3] = 0.7
        t[4] = -0.3
        seeds.append(t)

    return seeds


def theta_score_grad(theta, B, tau, L, k_direct=32):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    eta = B.dot(theta)
    p = stable_softmax(eta)
    score, grad_eta = score_grad(p, tau, L, int(k_direct))
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return p, np.inf, np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    grad_theta = B.T.dot(grad_eta)
    grad_theta = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_theta), grad_theta, 0.0)
    return p, score, grad_theta


def theta_adam_phase(theta0, B, deadline, rng, tau_ratio0=0.18, tau_ratio1=0.04,
                     lr0=0.10, lr1=0.04, k_direct=32, label="theta"):
    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = B.shape[0]
    D = B.shape[1]
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf, theta
    if D == 0:
        p = stable_softmax(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))
        return p, fast_score(p), theta

    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    p = stable_softmax(B.dot(theta))
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(p)
    best_theta = theta.copy()

    m = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    adam_step = 0
    step_count = 0
    no_improve = 0

    phase_start = time.monotonic()
    phase_len = max(1e-9, deadline - phase_start)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        frac = (now - phase_start) / phase_len
        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, frac))
        tau = (tau_ratio0 + (tau_ratio1 - tau_ratio0) * frac) / float(n)
        lr = lr0 + (lr1 - lr0) * frac

        p, score, gt = theta_score_grad(theta, B, tau, L, int(k_direct))
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break

        if score < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s = float(score)
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_theta = theta.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 10000 and score > best_s * 1.02:
            theta = jittered_theta(best_theta, D, rng, 0.35)
            m *= 0.25
            v *= 0.25
            adam_step = 0
            no_improve = 0

        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(gt))) if gt.size else 0.0
        if np.isfinite(maxg) and maxg > 1e-300:
            if maxg > 1e4:
                gt *= (1e4 / maxg)
            adam_step += 1
            m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * gt
            v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (gt * gt)
            b1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** adam_step
            b2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** adam_step
            theta = theta - lr * (m / b1) / (np.sqrt(v / b2) + eps)
            theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
            mt = float(np.max(np.abs(theta))) if theta.size else 0.0
            if mt > 20.0:
                theta *= (20.0 / mt)

        step_count += 1
        if step_count % 25000 == 0:
            print(label, "step", step_count, "score", score, "best", best_s)

    return best_p, best_s, best_theta


def low_dim_search(parent_p, deadline, rng, n, B, D):
    n = int(n)
    p0 = resize_sequence(parent_p, n) if parent_p is not None else None
    if p0 is None:
        p0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(p0)
    best_theta = project_log_to_basis(p0, B) if D > 0 else np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if D <= 0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return best_p, best_s, best_theta

    seeds = make_initial_seeds(best_theta, D, rng)
    idx = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if idx >= len(seeds):
            seeds.append(jittered_theta(best_theta, D, rng, 0.55))
            seeds.append(random_theta(D, rng, 1.25))

        theta = seeds[idx]
        idx += 1
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining <= 0.0:
            break

        left = max(1, len(seeds) - idx)
        if left > 14:
            dur = 2.5
        elif left > 5:
            dur = 4.0
        else:
            dur = min(8.0, remaining * 0.25)
        dur = max(1.0, min(float(dur), max(0.1, remaining - 0.5)))
        end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + dur)

        p, s, th = theta_adam_phase(
            theta, B, end, rng,
            tau_ratio0=0.20, tau_ratio1=0.05,
            lr0=0.11, lr1=0.04,
            k_direct=32, label="theta"
        )
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_s = float(s)
            best_theta = np.asarray(th, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print("low-dim improve", best_s)

        if idx % 8 == 0:
            print("low-dim seed", idx, "best", best_s)

    return best_p, best_s, best_theta


def adam_phase(p0, deadline, rng, tau_ratio0=0.15, tau_ratio1=0.05,
               lr0=0.045, lr1=0.025, k_direct=32, label="adam"):
    p = safe_normalize(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -50.0)
    z -= z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(p)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    adam_step = 0
    step_count = 0
    no_improve = 0

    phase_start = time.monotonic()
    phase_len = max(1e-9, deadline - phase_start)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        frac = (now - phase_start) / phase_len
        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, frac))
        tau = (tau_ratio0 + (tau_ratio1 - tau_ratio0) * frac) / float(n)
        lr = lr0 + (lr1 - lr0) * frac

        score, grad = score_grad(p, tau, L, int(k_direct))
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break

        if score < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s = float(score)
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 12000 and score > best_s * 1.03:
            q = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, n))
            q = safe_normalize(q)
            if q is None:
                q = best_p.copy()
            p = q
            z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
            z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -50.0)
            z -= z.mean()
            z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
            m *= 0.25
            v *= 0.25
            adam_step = 0
            no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 45000:
            q = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.30, n))
            q = safe_normalize(q)
            if q is None:
                q = best_p.copy()
            p = q
            z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
            z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -50.0)
            z -= z.mean()
            z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
            m *= 0.10
            v *= 0.10
            adam_step = 0
            no_improve = 0

        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
        if np.isfinite(maxg) and maxg > 1e-300:
            if maxg > 1e4:
                grad *= (1e4 / maxg)
            adam_step += 1
            m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
            v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
            b1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** adam_step
            b2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** adam_step
            z = z - lr * (m / b1) / (np.sqrt(v / b2) + eps)
            z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
            z -= z.mean()
            z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
            p = stable_softmax(z)

        step_count += 1
        if step_count % 25000 == 0:
            print(label, "step", step_count, "score", score, "best", best_s)

    return best_p, best_s


def fixed_scipy_objective(z, tau, n, L, k_direct):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    p = stable_softmax(z)
    score, grad = score_grad(p, tau, L, int(k_direct))
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros_like(p)
    return float(score), grad


def lbfz_attempt(p0, budget_padding, rng, tau_ratio, maxiter=80, maxfun=1200, k_direct=32):
    p = safe_normalize(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -50.0)
    z -= z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    tau = float(tau_ratio) / float(n)

    try:
        options = {
            "maxiter": int(maxiter),
            "maxfun": int(maxfun),
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-8,
            "disp": False,
        }
        res = scipy_minimize(
            fixed_scipy_objective,
            z,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            args=(tau, n, L, int(k_direct)),
            options=options,
        )
    except Exception:
        res = None

    if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        p2 = stable_softmax(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
        s2 = fast_score(p2)
        if np.isfinite(s2):
            return p2, float(s2)

    return p, fast_score(p)


def legendre_candidate(p=8191):
    try:
        a = np.zeros(int(p), dtype=np.float64)
        a[0] = 1.0
        half = (int(p) - 1) // 2
        for i in range(1, int(p)):
            if pow(i, half, int(p)) == 1:
                a[i] = 1.0
        return a
    except Exception:
        return None


def consider_candidate(cands, scores, arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if float(arr.sum()) < 0.05:
        return
    s = fast_score(arr)
    if np.isfinite(s):
        cands.append(arr.copy())
        scores.append(float(s))


def safe_evaluate(lst):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    return float(val) if np.isfinite(val) else np.inf


def apply_simple_transforms(best_p, best_s, N):
    p = safe_normalize(best_p)
    if p is None:
        return best_p, best_s
    N = int(N)

    for gam in (0.5, 0.75, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        try:
            arr = np.power(np.maximum(p, 0.0), gam)
        except Exception:
            continue
        best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, arr, N)

    uni = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    for t in (0.10, 0.25, 0.40):
        arr = (1.0 - t) * p + t * uni
        best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, arr, N)

    try:
        arr = np.sort(p)
        best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, arr, N)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_p, best_s


def finish_sequence(best_p, rng, hard_deadline):
    p = safe_normalize(best_p)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    N = p.size
    cands = []
    scores = []

    consider_candidate(cands, scores, p)

    scales = (1.0, 5.0, 20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0, 1000000.0)
    for S in scales:
        arr = np.minimum(p * float(S), 1000.0)
        consider_candidate(cands, scores, arr)

    if N <= 12288:
        targets = []
        t1 = int(N * 1.5)
        t2 = int(N * 2)
        if t1 > N and t1 <= 16384:
            targets.append(t1)
        if t2 > N and t2 <= 16384:
            targets.append(t2)
        for target in targets:
            arr = resize_sequence(p, target)
            if arr is not None:
                consider_candidate(cands, scores, arr)

    for gam in (0.5, 0.75, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        try:
            arr = np.power(np.maximum(p, 0.0), gam)
            consider_candidate(cands, scores, arr)
        except Exception:
            pass

    uni = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    for t in (0.10, 0.25, 0.40, 0.60):
        arr = (1.0 - t) * p + t * uni
        consider_candidate(cands, scores, arr)

    for frac in (0.45, 0.55, 0.65, 0.75, 0.85):
        m = int(N * frac)
        if 0 < m < N:
            try:
                thr = np.partition(p, N - m)[N - m]
                arr = np.where(p >= thr, p, 0.0)
                consider_candidate(cands, scores, arr)
                b = np.where(p >= thr, 1.0, 0.0)
                consider_candidate(cands, scores, b)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if N >= 8000 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 10.0:
        arr = legendre_candidate(8191)
        if arr is not None:
            consider_candidate(cands, scores, arr)

    consider_candidate(cands, scores, np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0)

    if not cands:
        return [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024

    scores_arr = np.asarray(scores, dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores_arr)
    top = []
    for idx in order:
        i = int(idx)
        if not np.isfinite(scores_arr[i]):
            continue
        top.append(cands[i])
        if len(top) >= 4:
            break

    final_fast = top[0]
    final_seq = [float(x) for x in np.clip(final_fast, 0.0, 1000.0)]

    eval_count = 0
    for arr in top:
        if eval_count >= 2:
            break
        if arr.size > 10000:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 5.0:
            break
        lst = [float(x) for x in np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)]
        sc = safe_evaluate(lst)
        eval_count += 1
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            final_seq = lst
            break

    return final_seq


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = max(60.0, min(budget, 2400.0))

    hard_deadline = start + max(60.0, budget - 10.0)
    search_deadline = start + max(30.0, budget - 130.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**31))
    N_HIGH = 8192

    best_p = None
    best_s = np.inf

    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        hs = None

    if hs is not None:
        hs = np.where(np.isfinite(hs), hs, 0.0)
        hs = np.maximum(hs, 0.0)
        if hs.size > 0:
            best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, hs, N_HIGH)

    if best_p is None or not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best_p = np.ones(N_HIGH, dtype=np.float64) / float(N_HIGH)
        best_s = 2.0

    print("initial best", best_s)

    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = search_deadline - now

    if remaining > 120.0:
        low_n = 4096 if budget > 1500.0 else 2048
        D = 40 if low_n >= 4096 else 30
        B_low = cheb_basis(low_n, D)
        low_budget = min(150.0, max(30.0, remaining * 0.12))
        low_deadline = min(search_deadline, time.monotonic() + low_budget)
        low_p, low_s, _ = low_dim_search(best_p, low_deadline, rng, low_n, B_low, D)
        if low_p is not None:
            best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, resize_sequence(low_p, N_HIGH), N_HIGH)
        best_p, best_s = apply_simple_transforms(best_p, best_s, N_HIGH)
        print("after low-dim search", best_s)

    if HAVE_MINIMIZE and time.monotonic() + 60.0 < search_deadline:
        p_lb, s_lb = lbfz_attempt(best_p, None, rng, 0.14, maxiter=80, maxfun=1200, k_direct=32)
        if p_lb is not None:
            best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, p_lb, N_HIGH)
        best_p, best_s = apply_simple_transforms(best_p, best_s, N_HIGH)
        print("after early L-BFGS", best_s)

    high_end = search_deadline
    if high_end - time.monotonic() > 150.0:
        h_start = time.monotonic()
        h_len = max(1.0, high_end - h_start)

        e1 = h_start + 0.38 * h_len
        p1, _ = adam_phase(best_p, e1, rng, 0.16, 0.05, 0.050, 0.025, 32, "high1")
        if p1 is not None:
            best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, p1, N_HIGH)
        best_p, best_s = apply_simple_transforms(best_p, best_s, N_HIGH)
        print("after high1", best_s)

        if time.monotonic() < high_end:
            noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, N_HIGH))
            p2parent = safe_normalize(best_p * noise)
            if p2parent is None:
                p2parent = best_p.copy()
            e2 = h_start + 0.72 * h_len
            p2, _ = adam_phase(p2parent, e2, rng, 0.08, 0.02, 0.038, 0.018, 32, "high2")
            if p2 is not None:
                best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, p2, N_HIGH)
            best_p, best_s = apply_simple_transforms(best_p, best_s, N_HIGH)
            print("after high2", best_s)

        if time.monotonic() < high_end - 30.0:
            std = 0.12 if best_s > 1.55 else 0.05
            noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, std, N_HIGH))
            p3parent = safe_normalize(best_p * noise)
            if p3parent is None:
                p3parent = best_p.copy()
            p3, _ = adam_phase(p3parent, high_end, rng, 0.03, 0.006, 0.024, 0.008, 24, "high3")
            if p3 is not None:
                best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, p3, N_HIGH)
            best_p, best_s = apply_simple_transforms(best_p, best_s, N_HIGH)
            print("after high3", best_s)

    if HAVE_MINIMIZE and time.monotonic() + 45.0 < search_deadline:
        p_lb, s_lb = lbfz_attempt(best_p, None, rng, 0.035, maxiter=80, maxfun=1200, k_direct=24)
        if p_lb is not None:
            best_p, best_s = try_update(best_p, best_s, p_lb, N_HIGH)
        best_p, best_s = apply_simple_transforms(best_p, best_s, N_HIGH)
        print("after late L-BFGS", best_s)

    final_seq = finish_sequence(best_p, rng, hard_deadline)
    return final_seq
```
